- **tms-01-research**: Narrow the codebase down to the exact facts a task needs, as-is, with no opinions or recommendations. Use after a ticket exists and before design. - **tms-02-design**: Design the solution and prove it on paper before the first line of code, for every task regardless of size. Use after research is complete. Do not use to break work into phases; that is tms-03-plan. ([TmsNine/tms-pipeline](https://github.com/TmsNine/tms-pipeline/tree/HEAD/codex-skills/tms-02-design) · 2 copies)
- **tms-03-plan**: Break an approved design into small isolated phases with an exact file list before any code is written. Use after the owner approved the design. - **tms-04-implement**: Write the code for an approved plan, phase by phase, TDD-first, with the stage agent itself as the single executor of the whole chain. Use after the lead signed the plan. - **tms-04b-review**: Iterative independent code review of a task's active changes using fresh read-only reviewer agents that never inherit the orchestrator's conversation history. Use after implementation. Produces docs/TASK-ID/04b_review.md. Never decides whether the task passes and never returns the task to an earlier stage on its own. ([TmsNine/tms-pipeline](https://github.com/TmsNine/tms-pipeline/tree/HEAD/codex-skills/tms-04b-review) · 2 copies)
